PSScriptAnalyzer clean over `scripts/` after ASCII-only comment fix in `pre-commit.Tests.ps1`.

- **RollingLog UTF-8 preamble fix** — `RollingLog` wrote both `StreamWriter`s (fresh create + append reopen) with `System.Text.Encoding.UTF8`, which in .NET 10 is a BOM-emitting `UTF8Encoding` (preamble EF BB BF). `_currentFileSize` is tracked from `Encoding.UTF8.GetByteCount` and never counted that preamble, so tracked size diverged from the real file size (rotation could drift up to 3 bytes past the cap; an append reopen could inject a preamble at EOF). Fix: construct the writers with `new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: false)` (documented UTF-8 without preamble); byte accounting unchanged. Regression `[Fact] Log_file_tracks_utf8_byte_count_exactly_without_preamble` asserts fresh-create size equals the exact UTF-8 line byte count and stays exact after an append reopen (fails by 3 bytes pre-fix, verified by disposable spike).
- **RollingLog failure latch made volatile (pending commit)** — `RollingLog.Failed` was a manifest auto-property read lock-free by `IsFailed` (diagnostics) while every write happened under `_gate`, so a cross-thread reader had no memory-visibility guarantee for the latched flag. Replaced the auto-property with `private volatile bool _failed` and updated all six read/write sites (`IsFailed`, the two `WriteCore` guards, the `MarkFailed` write, and the `RotateIfNeeded` guard), preserving the existing locking discipline (writes under `_gate`, constructor excepted). Evidence: Core tests 73/73 PASS; `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` → 12/12 PASS (228.6 s, 2026-09-10).

Fixes: (1) `CONTRIBUTING.md` prerequisites name Visual Studio 2026 (18) Community as the verification IDE (ADR-0003) and the review process now requires the branch pushed + GitHub CI observed green (W-12) before merge — a local `verify.ps1` PASS alone is insufficient. (5) `docs/06-LLM-PROTOCOL.md` worked evidence corrected to the verified 11-warning count recorded in `docs/STATUS.md` / `docs/KNOWN-LIMITATIONS.md` L10 (was 10), with the discrepancy reported rather than reconciled. - **AddInAssemblyTests Debug fallback restored (8927f2f)** — `AddInAssemblyTests.Configurations` had been narrowed to `["Release"]` in `f4d79da` while the comment still described a Debug fallback and the exception message still named `{Release|Debug}` — both lies. The silently-broken fallback also pulled `AddIn_assembly_loads_successfully` and `AddIn_targets_windows_TFM` into requiring a Release build even though only `AddIn_packaged_xll_exists` needs the packed XLL (a Release publish artifact). Fix: restore `Configurations` to `["Release", "Debug"]` (Release preferred) matching the `d0c6c8e` design goal, extract `LocateAddInBinDirectory()` so the XLL test resolves the Release publish path directly, and align the comment + exception message with what the code actually does. Evidence: `dotnet test --configuration Debug` → AddIn.Tests 3/3 (XLL test passes because Release publish output exists from prior verify run); `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` → 12/12 PASS. Note: `IDE1006` (private field `_` prefix) fires on `Configurations` but is pre-existing — every `private static readonly` field in the codebase uses PascalCase, so this is a `.editorconfig`-vs-convention mismatch, out of scope here.

- **AGENTS.md trim + skill-tree references.md removal (pending commit)** — Token-footprint audit of `.cline/skills/` and `AGENTS.md` found two redundancies: (1) `AGENTS.md` (loaded every turn) substantially restated content already owned in full by skills `02-architecture`, `04-test-strategy`, `05-git-quality`, and `06-llm-protocol`; (2) the per-skill `references.md` copy was a byte-for-byte duplicate of the matching `docs/0N-*.md`, generated and drift-checked for zero information `SKILL.md`'s own "Full reference: docs/..." footer didn't already provide. Fixes: `AGENTS.md` condensed (sections that elaborate a rule now end with a one-line pointer to the owning skill instead of restating it; the non-negotiable product invariants and the validator-positive-test rule kept verbatim; the orphaned C# conventions moved into a new `docs/02-ARCHITECTURE.md` "C# and architecture conventions" section, the testing principles into `docs/04-TEST-STRATEGY.md` "Test objective", and the control-record discipline into `docs/05-GIT-QUALITY.md` "Control-record discipline" so every AGENTS.md pointer resolves). `scripts/sync-cline-skills.ps1` no longer generates `references.md`; all 7 committed `references.md` files were deleted. Each `docs/0N-*.md` gained a hand-authored `<!-- SKILL-SUMMARY:START/END -->` block (5-15 lines) that the sync script extracts verbatim for `SKILL.md`, replacing the "first 80 non-empty lines" truncation heuristic that had already once silently dropped load-bearing content (the reason `check-skill-summary.ps1` existed). The `SKILL-SUMMARY` block is now **required**: a canonical doc missing it fails the sync with a non-zero exit, consistent with `docs/clinerules/SYNC.md`; the truncation fallback is removed. `scripts/sync-cline-skills.Tests.ps1` updated (references.md-existence assertion removed; new assertions: no `references.md` created, SKILL-SUMMARY block extracted verbatim, footer points at the canonical doc, no "references.md" mention) and its fixture doc gained a SKILL-SUMMARY block; the `$map`-injection regex in both `sync-cline-skills.Tests.ps1` and `check-cline-skills.Tests.ps1` hardened from a lookahead form that swallowed the script's new variable declarations to a line-anchored `(?ms)\$map = @\{.*?^\}` form. `scripts/check-cline-skills.ps1` needed no change — its drift check is a generic regenerate-and-diff, not a hardcoded file-name check. `check-skill-summary.ps1` + `check-skill-summary.Tests.ps1` deleted (their hardcoded phrase checks patched the truncation failure mode the SKILL-SUMMARY block eliminates); the 'skill summary phrases' step removed from `verify-quick.ps1` (now 11 steps) and `verify.ps1` (now 14 steps), and the corresponding pre-commit-hook checker entry removed from `scripts/pre-commit.ps1` (3 gates now: skill-tree drift, status accuracy, markdown links) with `pre-commit.Tests.ps1` updated to the 3-checker contract (including a new negative assertion that the hook must NOT invoke `check-skill-summary.ps1`). `docs/clinerules/SYNC.md` updated to describe the one-file-per-skill shape and the `SKILL-SUMMARY` convention. `.cline/skills/0N-*/SKILL.md` regenerated (7 files, ~1.0-1.4 KB each; summaries 100% hand-authored). Follow-up hardening: the `02-architecture` SKILL-SUMMARY block gained a "do not get wrong" bullet naming the doc's new "C# and architecture conventions" section as binding for every C# change, and the `02-architecture` skill description in `sync-cline-skills.ps1` was extended to trigger on C# conventions conversations — without this, an agent that only loaded the summary would never learn the relocated C# rules existed. Token-budget round: all 7 skill descriptions compressed (326 → 235 words, ~70 tokens/turn off the constant always-on budget) by dropping the "Use this skill when the conversation is about" boilerplate and moving to keyword-forward "Trigger on ..." forms with every trigger keyword retained; and the `04-test-strategy` / `05-git-quality` summaries now deliver their relocated AGENTS.md one-liner rules verbatim in the summary (observable-behaviour + deterministic/parallel-safe; comment-value + control-record discipline) so those skills need no second hop when triggered. Evidence: `pwsh ./scripts/test-scripts.ps1` → 65/65 Pester PASS; `pwsh ./scripts/sync-cline-skills.ps1` → "regenerated 7 skills", no fallback warnings; `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` → 11 steps, all PASS (110.1 s, 2026-09-08; includes the drift gate against the regenerated tree, PSScriptAnalyzer clean after UTF-8 BOMs were added to the three edited non-ASCII scripts, and the full non-Office test suite).

- **Phase D review methodology (2026-09-07)** — W-7 from the QA/QC fix programme. (1) `docs/06-LLM-PROTOCOL.md` gains an "End-to-end code review methodology" section that codifies the six-phase protocol demonstrated by the W-13 / Phase A/B/C run on 2026-09-07 (read the source, probe empirically, identify defect classes, plan, implement+verify+commit, self-application), with the four observed defect classes named (Gate integrity / Build pipeline drift / Docs/code drift / Local/CI view divergence) and the W-8/W-9/W-10/W-11/W-12/W-13 amendments cited as the worked examples. (2) New `scripts/review-runbook.Tests.ps1` (6 Pester tests) is the self-test for the methodology: it asserts the methodology section exists, the four defect classes are named, the W-13 capture-then-judge pattern is in `docs/04-TEST-STRATEGY.md`, the four worked-example tests (`pssa-gate`, `step-parity`, `ArtifactSourceMarker`, `ci-parity`) exist as files, `scripts/tool-versions.psd1` is read by `ci.yml` + `lint-ci.ps1` + `test-scripts.ps1` (the W-11 single source of truth has three consumers), and the W-12 branch policy is in `docs/05-GIT-QUALITY.md`. A future contributor who removes any of these artefacts or the supporting gate test fails this runbook test, which forces a conversation rather than a silent regression. (3) Skill regeneration: `docs/04-TEST-STRATEGY.md` gained "Gate integrity (W-13)" and "Step-parity (W9)" subsections; the skill copy under `.cline/skills/` is regenerated so the next agent gets the same rules. Evidence: `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` 12/12 PASS (post-Phase-C run, 117.5 s); the review-runbook test suite is itself 6/6 Pester and now runs as part of every `test-scripts.ps1` invocation. Two observed green GitHub CI runs of `stage-inspect` are the closing evidence; the next push (which will include this Phase D commit) is the third, the strongest signal that the methodology produced durable gates.
- **Phase C QA/QC amendments (2026-09-07)** — W8 / W9 / W10 / W11 / W12 from the QA/QC fix programme. (1) **W11 single source of truth**: new `scripts/tool-versions.psd1` pins Pester minimum major (5), PSScriptAnalyzer 1.25.0, and the actionlint 1.7.7 SHA-256 + download URL; `lint-ci.ps1` and `test-scripts.ps1` now import it instead of hard-coding the values, and `ci.yml`'s PSScriptAnalyzer install line reads the version from the same source. `ci-parity.Tests.ps1` has three new Pester tests asserting the SHA-256 in `ci.yml` and `lint-ci.ps1` equals the psd1 (closes L9). (2) **W8 full step allow-list**: `ci-parity.Tests.ps1` now also asserts `dotnet format` is a native allow-listed step and no bare `dotnet test` exists outside the verify-script delegation contract; the original 3 tripwires are preserved. (3) **W9 step-parity + no-silent-pass**: new `scripts/verify-helpers.ps1` extracts the verify scripts' `Invoke-Step` names and `.DESCRIPTION` numbering; new `scripts/step-parity.Tests.ps1` asserts they match (description-vs-actual drift becomes a gate failure). `check-md-links.ps1` now fails when the scan finds zero markdown files (a no-silent-pass fix — the original code returned OK on an empty scan, which proves nothing about link health). (4) **W10 artifact-traceability enforcement**: new `tests/GanttCreator.Architecture.Tests/ArtifactSourceMarkerTests.cs` scans every `tests/**/bin` or `publish` reference and requires a `// artifact-source: <step>` marker; positive control test ensures the regex + marker contract stays in sync. (5) **W12 review/process policy**: `docs/REVIEW-CHECKLIST.md` now has CI-parity, artifact-traceability, and reviewer-rule sections (validator-with-positive-test, culture-roundtrip, `TimeProvider` boundary); `docs/05-GIT-QUALITY.md` adds the CI-parity section and a branch-policy rule that phases may not exit on local-only evidence (the W-12 amendment motivated by the PSSA blind-gate surviving three rounds without CI). Evidence: `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` 12/12 PASS (113.7 s, 2026-09-07); Pester 51/51 (44 prior + 2 step-parity + 2 ci-parity version + 3 tool-versions); C# tests Core 65/65, Raster 37/37, Architecture 15/15, AddIn 3/3; zero PSSA warnings. First green GitHub CI run of `stage-inspect` (after the W13 PSSA fix) is the closing evidence for the W12 branch-policy rule.
- **PSScriptAnalyzer blind-gate fix (2026-09-07)** — The first CI push of `stage-inspect` failed at the PSScriptAnalyzer step (exit 11) under PSScriptAnalyzer 1.25.0 with 11 warnings in `scripts/*.Tests.ps1`. Investigation found that `Invoke-ScriptAnalyzer -EnableExit` calls `exit` from inside a function, which does not propagate to the parent process's $LASTEXITCODE when the cmdlet runs inside the Tee/ForEach pipeline in `Invoke-Step` — so the local analyzer step was silently blind: every "PSScriptAnalyzer clean" claim since R0.8 was a false PASS. (1) The W-13 fix: both `verify-quick.ps1` and `verify.ps1` now capture findings and judge in the step's own scope instead of using `-EnableExit`. (2) The W-14 fix: 8 `PSUseDeclaredVarsMoreThanAssignment` warnings resolved by moving `$scriptPath`/`$installScriptPath` from a top-level `BeforeAll` (PSSA can't follow Pester's block-scoping) into a `Describe` `BeforeAll` that uses the `$script:` scope, and updating all inner references. 3 `PSUseShouldProcessForStateChangingFunctions` warnings resolved by renaming the state-mutating test helpers from `New-PreCommitStubChecker`, `New-PreCommitTerminatingErrorStubChecker`, and `New-LockedRestoreHarness` to `Write-*` (renaming out of the state-changing verb class is the documented PSSA escape; the helpers do not actually require `-Confirm` semantics). (3) The W-15 positive test: new `scripts/pssa-gate.Tests.ps1` asserts the step's anti-blind guarantee (no `-EnableExit` in `verify-quick.ps1`) and reproduces a real warning with a synthetic fixture. (4) The W-16 docs: this entry + L10 in `KNOWN-LIMITATIONS.md`. Evidence: `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` 12/12 PASS (114.2 s, 2026-09-07); Pester 41/41 (40 prior + 1 new); zero PSSA warnings.
- **Phase B correctness round (2026-09-07)** — W4/W5/W6 from the QA/QC fix programme. (1) W4 Core: `VersionInfo.ExtractSemanticVersion` now validates semver numeric parts character-by-character against ASCII digits (rejecting signs, whitespace, leading zeros, and empty prerelease; culture-invariant), `SemanticVersion` documented as retaining a prerelease suffix, and the `SemanticVersion` test regex widened accordingly (`VersionInfoTests` 9 new assertions + de-DE culture test). `ExportSize` rejects non-positive widths at parse and a zero request value in `ToPixels`, and enforces a practical `MaxPixelDimension` of 65,535 px on both computed dimensions (`ExportSizeTests` 8 new cases). `RollingLog` injects `TimeProvider` (AGENTS clock-boundary rule), matches rotated files only as exact `{base}.{index}.log` with ASCII-digit indexes so prefix-sharing foreign files (`{base}2.log`, `{base}-backup.log`) are never shifted or deleted, removes the dead `_currentFilePath` field, converts `_failed` to an auto property behind a new public `IsFailed`, and contains disposal errors in `Dispose` (`RollingLogTests` 4 new tests incl. fixed-clock timestamp and de-DE culture test for the format overload). (2) W5: `Redactor`'s Unix path pattern gained a leading lookbehind so prose slashes (`and/or`, `3/4`, `12/31/2026`) are no longer redacted as `[path]`, with a behaviour table pinning both masked and unmasked cases (`RedactorTests` 5 new assertions). (3) W6: `check-md-links.ps1` anchors its roots to the repository root, uses `-LiteralPath` throughout, and now validates dot-prefixed relative links of the form dot-slash-name (a leading dot no longer exempts a link); `lint-ci.ps1` verifies the actionlint SHA-256 exactly as CI does; `CheckStatusScriptTests.cs` text tripwires were replaced by behavioural child-pwsh tests including both traversal-rejection cases; new `scripts/check-md-links.Tests.ps1` (4 tests) and `scripts/lint-ci.Tests.ps1` (2 anti-drift tests). Finding fixed during the round: the first version of the rotation matcher threw on the active `base.log` (prefix/suffix overlap inverted the slice range), latched the logger, and was caught only by the `Rotation_creates_new_file_when_size_exceeded` ≥2-files assertion — the one-sided "≤ cap" assertions passed with a single file. Evidence: Core 65/65, Raster 37/37, Architecture 13/13, Pester 40/40, `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` 12/12 PASS (2026-09-07).
- **Phase A QA fixes (2026-09-07)** — `7b0c2d9`, `071e9f1`, `d0c6c8e` fix three blocking/latent gate defects found in a full line-by-line code review. (1) `7b0c2d9`: the CI Pester step inlined a Pester call using the `-Script` parameter (removed in Pester 5) and would have failed the first CI run; the step now delegates to `scripts/test-scripts.ps1` (verify-parity restored) and `scripts/ci-parity.Tests.ps1` tripwires against inline workflow Pester logic, with a positive control for the step-extraction helper. (2) `071e9f1`: verify-script `Invoke-Step` clears `$LASTEXITCODE` per step (stale-exit false-PASS); `verify.ps1` lost the phantom SBOM step claim and the incorrect coverage-comment wording (docs/code drift, checklist I); `test-scripts.ps1` now uses the Pester 5/6-safe `PassedCount`/`FailedCount`/`TotalCount` — the previous `.TestResult` accounting reported `Total=0` under Pester 6 while 32 tests ran — and fails when no test files are found or a discovered file contains zero tests. (3) `d0c6c8e`: `AddInAssemblyTests`/`CoreBoundaryTests` locate Release or Debug build output so plain `dotnet test` is green on a clean tree, both carry `artifact-source` markers per the build-pipeline artifact contract (first increment of W10 traceability), and the `.vscode` `test` task depends on `publish-addin` with a positive `VscodeTasksTests` assertion. Evidence: `pwsh ./scripts/verify-quick.ps1` 12/12 PASS (97.2 s, 2026-09-07); Pester 32/32; Architecture 16/16; AddIn.Tests 3/3.

- **QA hardening round 3 (2026-09-07)** — Closes R0.8. (1) `7d69419` rewrote four script-test files (`check-cline-skills.Tests.ps1`, `check-status.Tests.ps1`, `check-skill-summary.Tests.ps1`, `sync-cline-skills.Tests.ps1`) plus `test-locked-restore.Tests.ps1` from fragile text-level regex assertions to an isolated child-pwsh harness that exercises the script's observable behaviour (exit code + script-written output); added `docs/evidence/script-test-harness-refactor.md`. (2) `5e20712` fixed the pre-commit `$LASTEXITCODE`-reset bug: piping the checker through `ForEach-Object`/`Write-Host` reset the exit code to 0, and a terminating `Write-Error` aborted the parent before the blocking message could print — both bugs combined silently masked `check-cline-skills.ps1`'s terminating-error path. The new try/catch wrapper captures `$LASTEXITCODE` before any other native command runs and converts terminating errors into exit 1 with the exception message. (3) `13b0f56` widened `IRedactor.Redact` to accept and return `string?` with `[NotNullIfNotNull(nameof(input))]`; `RollingLog.WriteCore` coalesces a null redactor result to `string.Empty`. (4) `c13a21a` clarified the drift-gate Phase 1 wording to "unstaged or untracked changes (staged changes are allowed)" and corrected the `install-pre-commit.ps1` `.DESCRIPTION` to match the actual `.githooks/` repository-root install path. Pester 28/28, Core 45/45, format clean, PSScriptAnalyzer clean.
- **R0.8 follow-up (2026-09-07)** — `d4b36fe` fixes a cap-violating branch in `RollingLog.PerformRotation`: when `_maxFileCount == 1` the active `.log` is now deleted instead of moved to `.1.log`, so the directory never holds two files for a cap of one. Behaviour for `maxFileCount >= 2` is unchanged. Paired with `[Fact] File_count_cap_of_one_deletes_active_log_on_rotation` in `RollingLogTests` (positive test, 10 rotations under cap-1, asserts no `.1.log` is ever created and total files `<= 1`). Failure containment is preserved: the new branch executes inside the existing `RotateIfNeeded` try/catch, so any I/O failure disables further writes via `_failed`. Core 46/46, Pester 28/28, `verify-quick.ps1` 12/12 PASS (201.4 s).

- **Review-hardening round (2026-09-08)** — Closes the review-findings batch across four commits. (1) `2c1519e` marked `SECURITY.md` pre-release (no released version exists), corrected the `verify-quick.ps1` duration claim in `docs/05-GIT-QUALITY.md` + skill mirrors to the measured `~2 minutes`, and scoped the `docs/06-LLM-PROTOCOL.md` validator positive-test rule to behavior changes that add validators (doc-only changes use doc/link/drift gates). (2) `dc3c41d` delegated the CI `dotnet build`/`dotnet publish`/`dotnet test` steps to shared entry points `scripts/build-release.ps1`, `scripts/publish-addin.ps1`, `scripts/test-non-office.ps1` (same entry points `verify-quick.ps1`/`verify.ps1` call, W8 local/CI parity); hardened the `ci-parity.Tests.ps1` inline-dotnet tripwire to reject every inline `dotnet` gate command (test/build/publish) with arguments and in multiline `run:` blocks, allowing only the vetted native commands `dotnet format`/`dotnet restore --locked-mode`; rewrote `install-pre-commit.ps1` to use the cross-platform `pwsh` shim and `git -C $repoRoot config core.hooksPath .githooks`; changed `pre-commit.ps1` blocking message from `Write-Error` to `Write-Host` (retaining `exit $checkerExit`). (3) `2555244` made `VersionInfo.ExtractSemanticVersion` a public contract and added prerelease-identifier validation (rejects empty identifiers like `alpha..1`, non-ASCII/non-hyphen chars, and numeric identifiers with leading zeros like `01`); `RollingLog` now exposes `IsFailed` through `IRollingLog`; renamed the rotation helper `GetRotatedFilesNewestFirst` → `GetRotatedFilesOldestFirst` with corrected XML docs (descending rotation index = oldest first) and removed the stale `int.MaxValue` comment. (4) `67a3a11` relocated the `pssa-gate.Tests.ps1` runner to a sibling temp directory (so `Invoke-PssaGate -Path` scans only fixtures) and added a W13-shape fixture asserting FAIL through the `Invoke-Step`/`Tee-Object`/`ForEach-Object` pipeline; extracted the actionlint SHA-256 check into `scripts/actionlint-hash.ps1` and added an offline child-process test to `lint-ci.Tests.ps1` asserting acceptance (matching hash) and rejection (mismatched hash). (5) `efcec33` added a pre-flight guard to `pre-commit.ps1` that refuses to run the checkers while the working tree is dirty under the inspected roots (`docs/`, `.cline/skills/`, `AGENTS.md`, `.github/`), the minimal correct alternative to per-checker `git show` index-snapshot refactoring: it guarantees working tree == index for the validated content so unstaged changes cannot mask issues in the committed content. Core 73/73, Pester 28/28, `ci-parity` 12/12, `pssa-gate` 4/4, `lint-ci` 4/4, `pre-commit` 9/9, format clean, PSScriptAnalyzer clean, `verify-quick.ps1` 12/12 PASS (332.8 s).
- **pre-commit dirty-tree guard repair (e5b5c67)** — The `pre-commit.ps1` dirty-tree guard was dead code: `$scriptRoot` resolves to `scripts/`, so `git -C $scriptRoot status -- docs .cline/skills AGENTS.md .github` looked for `scripts/docs` etc. (none exist) — `$dirty` was always empty and the guard never fired. Fixed by computing `$repoRoot = Split-Path -Parent $scriptRoot` and using `git -C $repoRoot`; verified empirically (unstaged AGENTS.md now correctly blocks with exit 1 + "Commit blocked"). Added two `pre-commit.Tests.ps1` cases in an isolated fixture repo (dirty → non-zero exit + "Commit blocked"; clean → exit 0 + "pre-commit: quick gates PASS"), three cases that would have caught the defects (dirty blocks; clean passes; staged-only passes). Pester 12/12 pre-commit.

- **CI PSSA step delegates to Invoke-PssaGate (pending commit)** — `ci.yml` PSSA step still called `Invoke-ScriptAnalyzer ... -EnableExit` directly instead of routing through `Invoke-PssaGate` (the entry point verify-quick.ps1/verify.ps1 use, hardened in W13). Fixed by dot-sourcing verify-helpers.ps1 and calling Invoke-PssaGate; added a ci-parity.Tests.ps1 `Describe 'PSScriptAnalyzer gate delegates to Invoke-PssaGate (W13)'` block asserting the step matches Invoke-PssaGate and (on code lines only, stripping full-line comments) does not match -EnableExit. Pester 13/13 ci-parity, 4/4 pssa-gate.

- **R1.2 evidence asymmetry (historical, not fixable; moved out of "Open questions for human" so that section stays consistent with its "none yet" state)** — The R1.2 Office-gate entry records "user-reported verification" without the edited filename or VS/Excel versions, unlike the detailed timestamped cross-corroborated entries for R1.3/R1.4. This asymmetry is permanent: the R1.2 regression fix already landed and the code moved on, so the entry cannot be retroactively strengthened. It is recorded here so an end-to-end audit of the evidence trail does not treat the gap as an omission.

- **R1.3 fix — Diagnostics button did nothing in Office; task-dialog swapped to
  managed WinForms API.** Evidence captured in a VS F5 session (one
  `System.EntryPointNotFoundException` per click, first exception read from the
  `.callback-error.txt` capture file next to the rolling log): the then hand-rolled
  P/Invoke declared `GetDesktopWindow` with `DllImport("comctl32.dll")`, but the
  entry point lives in user32.dll, so every click threw; comctl32 v6 exports
  `TaskDialogIndirect`/`TaskDialog` only by ordinal (121/119), never by name, so the
  same failure awaited after any by-name repair. Fix in
  `src/GanttCreator.AddIn/DiagnosticsService.TaskDialog.cs` (rewritten to the managed
  `System.Windows.Forms.TaskDialog` API: `TaskDialogPage`, `EnableLinks`, `LinkClicked`
  with `TaskDialogLinkClickedEventArgs.LinkHref`), `DiagnosticsService.cs`
  (MessageBox fallback with dedicated plain text carrying the log file path; `&`-escaping
  via `EscapeAccessKeys` because `EnableLinks` treats `&` as an access-key prefix;
  `WriteDiagnosticsError` now failure-proof through the rolling log), `GanttRibbon.cs`
  (one-line catch — the recorder is failure-proof by contract), and
  `<UseWindowsForms>true</UseWindowsForms>` in `GanttCreator.AddIn.csproj` and the test
  project (imports the .NET Desktop SDK — no new NuGet package; empirically needs no
  `TreatAsUsed`/analyzer tweak on `net10.0-windows` with `AnalysisMode=All` +
  warnings-as-errors: Release build 0 warnings). Regression facts for the page wiring,
  link delivery, fallback plain-text (log file path present), `&`-escaping, and positive error-path coverage in
  `tests/GanttCreator.AddIn.Tests/DiagnosticsServiceTests.cs`. Committed as
  `9ca4188` (fix), `77e28f3` (LF normalization for the whitespace gate), and
  `a92a583` (IDE0001/IDE0007 — note: the format gate is bare `dotnet format`,
  which includes style/analyzers that do not fire as build warnings).
  Gates: `dotnet test tests/GanttCreator.AddIn.Tests` 50/50 PASS;
  **`pwsh ./scripts/verify.ps1` → PASS in 154.4 s (all 14 steps)**; Office gate
  (F5, click Diagnostics, TaskDialog appears, hyperlink opens the log file, and no
  `EntryPointNotFoundException` in the VS Debug output): **PASS — confirmed by the
  human operator in VS 2026 F5 against desktop Excel (2026-09-13)**. Corroborating
  evidence from the session rolling log (gantt-creator-addin.log in the
  per-user log directory):
  every pre-fix `Diagnostics:` record (22:31–23:32 on 2026-09-12) has no

## Known limitations

- **L2** — `.vs/` (Visual Studio user options) is present locally and is already covered by `.gitignore`.
- **L3** — ~~Office fixture gap~~ **closed**. `OfficeFixture` harness exists; `OfficeFixtureTests.Excel_open_close_five_times_no_orphan` passes with no orphan `EXCEL.EXE` (5/5 Office integration tests).
- **L12** — ~~Blame-timeout limitation~~ **closed**. `verify-office.ps1` no longer passes `--blame-hang-timeout` (which aborted the testhost ~0.2 s after start); the deadline is enforced by an external watchdog that polls the owned `dotnet test` process and, on timeout, kills the whole owned process tree (`taskkill /PID /T /F`) and re-runs the harness-owned Office-process sweep as a safety net. Cleanup is guaranteed only for Office processes positively identified by the fixture manifest or by parentage in the owned `dotnet test` tree; when neither identifies a process, it is skipped and may remain. Covered by `scripts/verify-office.Tests.ps1` (no-flag assertion + hang/timeout and prompt-pass harness cases). Gates: `scripts/verify-office.Tests.ps1` 14/14 PASS; `scripts/verify-office.ps1` 8/8 PASS (27.8 s, no orphan); PSSA 0 findings on both modified scripts.
- **L8** — ~~Docstring coverage gap~~ **closed**. Documented decision (2026-09-04, human): not a project gate; rely on CS1591 + reviewer judgement.
